\displaystyle\lesssim_{m,d,D}C_{K}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}a_{j}\Big)\lambda^{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q_{t}\in\mathcal{F}_{r_{t}}\\
t=1,\ldots,\ell\end{subarray}}\int_{Q_{1}\times\ldots\times Q_{\ell}}\omega\Big(\frac{\ell(Q_{1})}{\sum_{j\in B}|x-y_{j}|}\Big)\frac{1}{\Big(\sum_{j\in B}|x-y_{j}|\Big)^{\#Bd}}\mathrm{d}y_{B}

\displaystyle\leq C_{K}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}a_{j}\Big)\lambda^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{\ell}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q_{t}\in\mathcal{F}_{r_{t}}\\
t=1,\ldots,\ell\\
\ell(Q_{k})=\max_{t=1,\ldots,\ell}\ell(Q_{t})\end{subarray}}\int_{Q_{1}\times\ldots\times Q_{\ell}}\omega\Big(\frac{\ell(Q_{k})}{\sum_{j\in B}|x-y_{j}|}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\Big(\sum_{j\in B}|x-y_{j}|\Big)^{\#Bd}}\mathrm{d}y_{B}

\displaystyle\leq C_{K}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}a_{j}\Big)\lambda^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{\ell}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q_{t}\in\mathcal{F}_{r_{t}}\\
t=1,\ldots,\ell\end{subarray}}\int_{Q_{1}\times\ldots\times Q_{\ell}}\omega\Big(\frac{\ell(Q_{k})}{\sum_{j\in B}|x-y_{j}|}\Big)\frac{1}{\Big(\sum_{j\in B}|x-y_{j}|\Big)^{\#Bd}}\mathrm{d}y_{B}

\displaystyle\leq C_{K}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}a_{j}\Big)\lambda^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{\ell}\sum_{Q_{k}\in\mathcal{F}_{r_{k}}}\int_{Q_{k}}\bigg(\int_{(\mathbf{R}^{d})^{B\setminus\{r_{k}\}}}\omega\Big(\frac{\ell(Q_{k})}{|x-y_{r_{k}}|}\Big)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Big(\sum_{j\in B}|x-y_{j}|\Big)^{\#Bd}}\mathrm{d}y_{B\setminus\{r_{k}\}}\bigg)\mathrm{d}y_{r_{k}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m,d}C_{K}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}a_{j}\Big)\lambda^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{\ell}\sum_{Q_{k}\in\mathcal{F}_{r_{k}}}\int_{Q_{k}}\omega\Big(\frac{\ell(Q_{k})}{|x-y_{r_{k}}|}\Big)\frac{1}{|x-y_{r_{k}}|^{d}}\mathrm{d}y_{r_{k}}.

Integrating over
x\in E
we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{E}|T(\vec{h})(x)|\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m,d,D}C_{K}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}a_{j}\Big)\lambda^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{\ell}\sum_{Q_{k}\in\mathcal{F}_{r_{k}}}\int_{Q_{k}}\Big(\int_{E}\omega\Big(\frac{\ell(Q_{k})}{|x-y_{r_{k}}|}\Big)\frac{1}{|x-y_{r_{k}}|^{d}}\mathrm{d}x\Big)\mathrm{d}y_{r_{k}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{d,D}C_{K}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}a_{j}\Big)\lambda^{m}\sum_{k=1}^{\ell}\sum_{Q_{k}\in\mathcal{F}_{r_{k}}}\int_{Q_{k}}\|\omega\|_{\mathrm{Dini}}\mathrm{d}y_{r_{k}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{K}\|\omega\|_{\mathrm{Dini}}\lambda^{m-1}\sum_{k=1}^{\ell}\bigg(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\\
j\neq k\end{subarray}}^{m}a_{j}\bigg)\|f_{k}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}.

Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle|\{|T(\vec{f})|>\lambda\}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m,d,D,\vec{q}}\frac{1}{\lambda}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{1}{a_{j}}\|f_{j}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\lambda^{q}}\|T\|_{L^{\vec{q}}(\mathbf{R}^{d})\to L^{q}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}^{q}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}(a_{j}\lambda)^{\frac{1}{q_{j}^{\prime}}}\Big)\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}\|f_{j}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}^{\frac{1}{q_{j}}}\Big)^{q}

\displaystyle+C_{K}\|\omega\|_{\mathrm{Dini}}\lambda^{m-1}\sum_{k=1}^{\ell}\bigg(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\\
j\neq k\end{subarray}}^{m}a_{j}\bigg)\|f_{k}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}.

We now choose

a_{j}:=\frac{\|f_{j}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}^{1-\frac{1}{m}}}{\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
k\neq j\end{subarray}}^{m}\|f_{k}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}^{\frac{1}{m}}}\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda^{1-\frac{1}{m}}}\cdot\frac{1}{(\|T\|_{L^{\vec{q}}(\mathbf{R}^{d})\to L^{q}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}+\|\omega\|_{\mathrm{Dini}})^{\frac{1}{m}}},\quad j=1,\ldots,m.

A direct computation shows that this choice yields the desired result.
∎

Proposition A.2
.

For any cube
Q_{0}\subseteq\mathbf{R}^{d}
one has

|T(\vec{f}\ind_{3Q_{0}})(x)|\leq c\|T\|_{L^{\vec{1}(\mathbf{R}^{d})\to L^{\frac{1}{m},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}}\prod_{j=1}^{m}|f_{j}(x)|+\mathcal{M}_{T,Q_{0}}(\vec{f})(x)

for a.e.
x\in Q_{0}
, where the constant
c>0
depends only on
d
.

Proof.

The proof given in
[
Li18
, Lemma 2.1]
for
m=2
works mutatis mutandis. One has to replace
2
by
m
,
\vec{q}
by
\vec{1}
,
q
by
1
,
(f_{1}\chi_{3Q_{0}},f_{2}\chi_{3Q_{0}})
by
\vec{f}\ind_{3Q_{0}}
,
(f_{1}\chi_{3Q},f_{2}\chi_{3Q})
by
\vec{f}\ind_{3Q}
,
(f_{1},f_{2})
by
\vec{f}
, and, at the very end of the proof, let
x
be a Lebesgue point of all
|f_{1}|,\ldots,|f_{m}|
.
∎

Proposition A.3
.

For all
\eta\in\Big(0,\frac{1}{m}\Big)
we have

\mathcal{M}_{T}(\vec{f})\leq C(C_{K}\|\omega\|_{\mathrm{Dini}}+\|T\|_{L^{\vec{1}}(\mathbf{R}^{d})\to L^{\frac{1}{m},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{d})})\mathcal{M}(\vec{f})+M_{\eta}(|T(\vec{f})|)\quad\text{a.e.\penalty\ on }\mathbf{R}^{d},

where the constant
C>0
depends only on
m,d,\eta
and the doubling constant of
\omega
.

Proof.

We adapt and combine the proofs of
[
Li18
, Theorem 1.2]
and
[
DHL18
, Theorem 6.6]
. Let
Q\subseteq\mathbf{R}^{d}
be any cube. Then, for all
x,x^{\prime},\xi\in Q
we have

\displaystyle|T(\vec{f})(\xi)-T(\vec{f}\ind_{3Q})(\xi)|=\Big|\int_{(\mathbf{R}^{d})^{m}\setminus(3Q)^{m}}K(\xi,\vec{y})\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}f_{j}(y_{j})\Big)\mathrm{d}\vec{y}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\int_{(\mathbf{R}^{d})^{m}\setminus(3Q)^{m}}(K(\xi,\vec{y})-K(x^{\prime},\vec{y}))\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}f_{j}(y_{j})\Big)\mathrm{d}\vec{y}\Big|+|T(\vec{f})(x^{\prime})|+|T(\vec{f}\ind_{3Q})(x^{\prime})|.

We estimate

\displaystyle\Big|\int_{(\mathbf{R}^{d})^{m}\setminus(3Q)^{m}}(K(\xi,\vec{y})-K(x^{\prime},\vec{y}))\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}f_{j}(y_{j})\Big)\mathrm{d}\vec{y}\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\int_{(2^{k}\cdot 3Q)^{m}\setminus(2^{k-1}\cdot 3Q)^{m}}|K(\xi,\vec{y})-K(x^{\prime},\vec{y})|\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}|f_{j}(y_{j})|\Big)\mathrm{d}\vec{y}

\displaystyle\leq C_{K}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\int_{(2^{k}\cdot 3Q)^{m}\setminus(2^{k-1}\cdot 3Q)^{m}}\omega\Big(\frac{|\xi-x^{\prime}|}{\sum_{j=1}^{m}|\xi-y_{j}|+\sum_{j=1}^{m}|x^{\prime}-y_{j}|}\Big)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{m}|\xi-y_{j}|+\sum_{j=1}^{m}|x^{\prime}-y_{j}|\Big)^{md}}\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}|f_{j}(y_{j})|\Big)\mathrm{d}\vec{y}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{m,d,D}C_{K}\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\omega\Big(\frac{1}{2^{k}}\Big)\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{m}\langle|f_{j}|\rangle_{2^{k}\cdot 3Q}\Big)\lesssim_{D}C_{k}\|\omega\|_{\mathrm{Dini}}\mathcal{M}(\vec{f})(x).

Now, taking an
L^{\eta}
-average over
x^{\prime}\in Q
, we obtain:

\displaystyle|T(\vec{f})(\xi)-T(\vec{f}\ind_{3Q})(\xi)|\lesssim_{\eta,m,d,D}C_{k}\|\omega\|_{\mathrm{Dini}}\mathcal{M}(\vec{f})(x)+\langle|T(\vec{f})|^{\eta}\rangle_{Q}^{\frac{1}{\eta}}+\langle|T(\vec{f}\ind_{3Q})|^{\eta}\rangle_{Q}^{\frac{1}{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{k}\|\omega\|_{\mathrm{Dini}}\mathcal{M}(\vec{f})(x)+M_{\eta}(|T(\vec{f})|)(x)+\langle|T(\vec{f}\ind_{3Q})|^{\eta}\rangle_{Q}^{\frac{1}{\eta}}.

Since

L^{\frac{1}{m},\infty}\subseteq L^{\eta}

because
0<\eta<\frac{1}{m}
, we deduce

\displaystyle\langle|T(\vec{f}\ind_{3Q})|^{\eta}\rangle_{Q}^{\frac{1}{\eta}}\lesssim_{\eta}\|T(\vec{f}\ind_{3Q})\|_{L^{\frac{1}{m},\infty}\Big(Q,\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{|Q|}\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|Q|^{m}}\|T\|_{L^{\vec{1}}(\mathbf{R}^{d})\to L^{\frac{1}{m},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}\prod_{j=1}^{m}\|f_{j}\ind_{3Q}\|_{L^{1}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{d,m}\|T\|_{L^{\vec{1}}(\mathbf{R}^{d})\to L^{\frac{1}{m},\infty}(\mathbf{R}^{d})}\mathcal{M}(\vec{f})(x),

concluding the proof.
∎
